==========================================================================================
HOW TO VERIFY MANUALLY IN WIRESHARK
==========================================================================================

1. Open Wireshark
2. File -> Open -> capture1.pcapng
3. Apply filter: udp.dstport == 9000
4. Go to packet #168:
   - Click on packet
   - Expand "Data" section at bottom
   - Look at bytes (hex view on right)
   - Bytes [8-9] = sequence number in little-endian
   - You should see: a9 00

5. Go to packet #169:
   - Same steps
   - Bytes [8-9] should show: aa 00

6. Note: There are NO packets between #168 and #169
   This proves the 170 packets were never received by your PC!
